To illustrate our results, numerical experiments based on a two dimensional fake stationary rough Heston model highlight the impact of rough volatilities and stochastic correlations on the optimal Markowitz strategies.

</details>


### [112] [Risk Control of Traffic Flow Through Chance Constraints and Large Deviation Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01321)
*Rui Xu,Shanyin Tong,Xuan Di*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于大偏差理论的罕见机会约束最优控制框架，用于自主交通管理，能高效处理安全关键极端事件


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有宏观交通控制方法难以在随机扰动下严格调控罕见但安全关键的极端事件，需要开发能有效处理概率安全约束的控制框架

Method: 利用大偏差理论（LDT）近似方法，将高度非凸、采样密集的优化问题转化为可处理的确定性非线性规划问题，实现计算可扩展性

Result: LDT重构方法具有优越的计算可扩展性，计算负担不随目标违反概率水平变化，避免了传统采样方法的极端扩展瓶颈，在精确近目标概率控制和计算效率方面优于风险规避基线

Conclusion: 提出的罕见机会约束最优控制框架能有效处理交通系统中的极端安全事件，在计算效率和概率控制精度方面优于现有方法，为自主交通管理提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: Existing macroscopic traffic control methods often struggle to strictly regulate rare, safety-critical extreme events under stochastic disturbances. In this paper, we develop a rare chance-constrained optimal control framework for autonomous traffic management. To efficiently enforce these probabilistic safety specifications, we exploit a large deviation theory (LDT) based approximation method, which converts the original highly non-convex, sampling-heavy optimization problem into a tractable deterministic nonlinear programming problem. In addition, the proposed LDT-based reformulation exhibits superior computational scalability, as it maintains a constant computational burden regardless of the target violation probability level, effectively bypassing the extreme scaling bottlenecks of traditional sampling-based methods. The effectiveness of the proposed framework in achieving precise near-target probability control and superior computational efficiency over risk-averse baselines is illustrated through extensive numerical simulations across diverse traffic risk measures.

</details>


### [113] [Output-Feedback Controller Synthesis for Dissipativity and $H_2$ Performance of Autoregressive Systems from Noisy Input-Output Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01347)
*Pietro Kristović,Andrej Jokić,Mircea Lazar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于数据的输出反馈控制器综合方法，用于离散时间线性时不变系统，目标为实现给定二次供给率下的耗散性或特定H₂性能水平，无需系统模型知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对离散时间线性时不变系统，当系统模型未知（仅知扰动项）时，需要一种基于数据而非模型的控制器设计方法。传统模型依赖方法在此类场景下无法应用，而实际应用中常可获得输入输出轨迹数据，这些数据可能受到未知但有界扰动的影响。

Method: 提出数据驱动的输出反馈控制器综合方法，系统采用特定自回归形式。利用受控输入和输出的记录轨迹（可能受未知有界扰动影响）进行控制器设计。将控制器综合问题转化为线性矩阵不等式形式，在考虑的问题设定下具有非保守性。

Result: 该方法能够实现两种控制目标：1）针对给定二次供给率实现耗散性；2）达到给定的H₂性能水平。所提方法以线性矩阵不等式形式呈现，在问题设定范围内具有非保守性，为模型未知系统提供了有效的控制器设计途径。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种基于数据的输出反馈控制器综合方法，适用于模型未知的离散时间线性时不变系统。该方法利用输入输出轨迹数据，通过线性矩阵不等式形式实现耗散性或H₂性能控制目标，在考虑的问题设定下具有非保守性，为数据驱动控制提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper we propose a data-driven output-feedback controller synthesis method for discrete-time linear time-invariant systems in a specific autoregressive form. The synthesis goal is either to achieve dissipativity with respect to a given quadratic supply rate, or to achieve given $H_2$ performance level. It is assumed that the model of the plant is unknown, except for the disturbance term. To compensate for the lack of model knowledge, we have a recorded trajectory of the controlled input and the output available for control, which can be corrupted by an unknown but bounded disturbance. Derived controller synthesis method is in the form of linear matrix inequalities and is nonconservative within the considered problem setting.

</details>


### [114] [Sterile mosquito release via intelligent proportional controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01355)
*Cédric Join,Luis Almeida,Michel Fliess*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该研究将模型无关控制、超局部模型和智能比例控制器应用于昆虫不育技术，以解决种群控制中的采样问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 昆虫不育技术是一种通过释放绝育雄性昆虫来控制害虫和病媒昆虫种群的方法，但需要有效的控制策略来优化实施效果

Method: 采用模型无关控制、超局部模型和智能比例控制器，这些方法已在多个领域证明有效，能够处理复杂的控制问题并解决关键的采样问题

Result: 通过计算机模拟展示了所提控制方法的有效性，并对结果进行了讨论

Conclusion: 模型无关控制、超局部模型和智能比例控制器为昆虫不育技术提供了一种有效的控制框架，首次解决了该领域的关键采样问题

Abstract: The Sterile Insect Technique (SIT) against insect pests and insect vectors consists of releasing males that have been previously sterilized in order to reduce or eliminate a specific wild population. We study this complex control question via model-free control, ultra-local models, and intelligent proportional controllers that have already proven their effectiveness in various fields. They permit addressing, perhaps for the first time, the essential sampling question. Computer simulations are displayed and discussed.

</details>


### [115] [Exponential Stabilization of Moving Shockwave in ARZ Traffic Model via Boundary Control: Explicit Gains and Arbitrary Decay Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01382)
*Mina Cao,Mamadou Diagne,Peipei Shang,Lei Yu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文为Aw-Rascle-Zhang交通流模型开发边界反馈控制，将交通拥塞稳定到预定义的激波平衡状态，通过激波位置坐标变换和Lyapunov函数方法实现任意指数衰减率的稳定控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通流模型中的拥塞激波现象需要有效控制策略来稳定交通状态，避免拥塞扩散。现有研究需要处理移动边界和激波位置变化带来的控制挑战，需要开发能够将系统稳定到预定义激波平衡的反馈控制方法。

Method: 1. 使用基于激波位置的移动坐标变换，将包含自由流和拥堵流区域的移动边界2×2双曲系统转换为无激波的4×4增广系统在固定域上；2. 应用改进的Lyapunov泛函（考虑激波扰动）；3. 设计边界反馈控制律；4. 通过数值模拟验证控制效果。

Result: 理论证明：在给定的反馈控制下，激波位置和系统状态在H^2范数意义下能够以任意指数衰减率稳定；数值模拟：验证了控制策略的有效性，展示了系统向预定义激波平衡的稳定过程。

Conclusion: 成功开发了针对ARZ交通流模型的边界反馈控制方法，通过坐标变换和Lyapunov分析，实现了对交通拥塞激波的稳定控制，能够将系统以任意指数衰减率稳定到预定义的激波平衡状态，为交通流控制提供了有效的理论框架。

Abstract: This paper develops boundary feedback controls to stabilize traffic congestion toward a predefined shock equilibrium in the Aw-Rascle-Zhang (ARZ) traffic flow model. We transform the corresponding moving-boundary $2\times2$ hyperbolic system, covering free and congested flow regimes, respectively, into a shock-free $4\times4$ augmented system on a fixed domain via shock-location-based moving coordinates. By applying the modified Lyapunov functionals concerning shock perturbation, we show that the shock position and the state of the system in $H^2$-norm can be stabilized with an arbitrary exponential decay rate via the given feedback controls. Finally, the stabilization results are demonstrated by numerical simulations.

</details>


### [116] [Joint Pricing and Innovation Control in Regulated Recycling-Rate Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01402)
*Bowen Xie,Yijin Gao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个受监管的随机扩散模型用于回收率，并通过回收投资和产品定价的动态过程，构建了生产与工艺创新的联合控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于解决系统管理者在产品定价决策与投资支出之间的权衡问题，同时考虑绿色经济法规下的回收率监管要求，为可持续生产提供理论框架。

Method: 方法包括：1）建立受监管的随机扩散模型描述回收率动态；2）构建无限时域折扣成本结构下的联合随机控制问题；3）引入Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程；4）严格证明HJB解与价值函数对应关系；5）在温和假设下分析HJB方程；6）进行数值实验和敏感性分析。

Result: 研究结果：1）成功解决了联合受监管的随机生产和工艺创新准入控制问题；2）建立了HJB方程与基础控制问题价值函数之间的对应关系；3）在系统参数的温和假设下分析了HJB方程；4）通过数值实验验证了HJB表征的可处理性；5）评估了参数条件的实际相关性。

Conclusion: 结论表明，通过HJB方程方法可以有效解决受监管的随机生产和工艺创新控制问题，为绿色经济法规下的可持续生产决策提供了可处理的数学框架，数值实验验证了方法的实用性和参数条件的实际意义。

Abstract: We introduce a regulated stochastic diffusion model for the recycling rate and formulate a joint control problem over production and process innovation via the dynamics of recycling investment and product pricing. The resulting stochastic control problem captures the system manager's trade-off between product-price decisions and investment expenditures under an infinite-horizon discounted cost structure. Owing to the recycling-rate specification, we incorporate two regulated state processes, which induce additional policy-driven cost components in the value function consistent with green-economy regulations. We resolve the jointly regulated stochastic production and process-innovation admission control problem by introducing the associated Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) equation and providing rigorous proofs that establish the correspondence between the HJB solution and the value function of the underlying control problem. The HJB equation is analyzed under mild, practically motivated assumptions on the system parameters. We further present numerical experiments and sensitivity analyses to illustrate the tractability of the HJB characterization and to assess the practical relevance of the imposed parameter conditions.

</details>


### [117] [Concentration of Stochastic System Trajectories with Time-varying Contraction Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01403)
*Zishun Liu,Liqian Ma,Hongzhe Yu,Yongxin Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出两种在时变收缩条件下非线性随机系统的集中不等式，基于平均矩生成函数(AMGF)方法，分别针对单时间点状态偏差和整个轨迹波动进行概率界分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非线性随机系统在时变收缩条件下的集中性行为，为随机系统分析提供概率保证，特别是在随机安全控制等应用中需要量化系统轨迹与确定性对应物之间的偏差。

Method: 引入平均矩生成函数(AMGF)作为能量函数，结合增量稳定性分析得到单时间点集中不等式；进一步将AMGF与鞅方法结合，推导出整个轨迹波动的集中不等式；通过综合两种结果，显著改进了强收缩系统的轨迹级集中不等式。

Result: 推导出两种集中不等式：1) 单时间点状态偏差的概率界；2) 整个随机轨迹波动的概率界。对于概率水平1-δ，确保随机轨迹偏差的界为$\mO(\sqrt{\log(1/δ))}$，该界在所给假设下是紧的。

Conclusion: 提出的AMGF方法为非线性随机系统在时变收缩条件下提供了有效的集中不等式分析框架，结果在随机安全控制案例中得到验证，为随机系统分析提供了概率保证工具。

Abstract: We establish two concentration inequalities for nonlinear stochastic system under time-varying contraction conditions. The key to our approach is an energy function termed Averaged Moment Generating Function (AMGF). By combining it with incremental stability analysis, we develop a concentration inequality that bounds the deviation between the stochastic system state and its deterministic counterpart. As this inequality is restricted to single time instance, we further combine AMGF with martingale-based methods to derive a concentration inequality that bounds the fluctuation of the entire stochastic trajectory. Additionally, by synthesizing the two results, we significantly improve the trajectory-level concentration inequality for strongly contractive systems. Given the probability level $1-δ$, the derived inequalities ensure an $\mO(\sqrt{\log(1/δ))}$ bound on the deviation of stochastic trajectories, which is tight under our assumptions. Our results are exemplified through a case study on stochastic safe control.

</details>


### [118] [On the critical time of observability of the multi-dimensional Baouendi-Grushin equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01419)
*Jérémi Dardé,Mathilda Trabut*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了Baouendi-Grushin方程在张量化域上的可观测性，精确计算了最小可观测时间T*，并建立了T>T*时的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Baouendi-Grushin方程在张量化域上的可观测性性质，特别是确定内部观测和边界观测的最小可观测时间。

Method: 结合Carleman估计在环域或整个边界上建立精细的可观测性不等式，并采用Lebeau-Robbiano策略来定位观测集。

Result: 精确计算了张量化观测集的最小可观测时间T*，并证明了当T>T*时系统的可观测性。

Conclusion: 成功建立了Baouendi-Grushin方程在张量化域上的可观测性理论，为内部和边界观测提供了精确的最小时间阈值。

Abstract: We investigate the observability properties of the Baouendi-Grushin equation on a tensorized domain $Ω:= \mathcal{B}_R \times \tilde Ω$, where $\mathcal{B}_R$ is the open ball of radius $R$ in dimension $d \ge 2$, and $\tilde Ω$ is a smooth, bounded, open set of arbitrary dimension. Our main result is a precise calculation of the minimal observability time $T^*$, for tensorized observation sets of the form $ω\times \tilde Ω$, with $ω\subset \mathcal{B}_R$ (internal observation), and $Γ\times \tilde Ω$, with $Γ\subset \partial \mathcal{B}_R$ (boundary observation).
  The main novelty regards the sufficient condition, that is observability of the system when $T>T^*$. This is established by combining refined observability inequalities on the annulus--or the entire boundary--using Carleman estimates, together with a Lebeau-Robbiano strategy to localize the observation sets.

</details>


### [119] [Rendezvous Planning from Sparse Observations of Optimally Controlled Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01428)
*Thomas A. Scott,Lukas Taus,Yen-Hsi Richard Tsai,Tan Bui-Thanh,Justin G. R. Delva*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出概率框架解决会合规划问题：基于稀疏噪声观测估计快速移动目标轨迹，为较慢的搜寻智能体规划时空会合点，通过贪心最小化失败概率进行序列决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在稀疏噪声观测条件下，为速度较慢的智能体规划与快速移动目标的会合问题。这类问题在无人机加油、航天器服务、水面自主航行器操作、搜救任务和导弹防御等自主会合应用中普遍存在。

Method: 1) 使用滤波方法结合核最大后验估计和高斯过程校正来估计目标轨迹，生成轨迹假设的混合分布；2) 基于该估计选择最大化成功会合概率的时空会合点；3) 通过贪心最小化当前置信空间中的失败概率进行序列决策；4) 每次尝试失败后更新置信空间，确保后续决策基于统计一致的剩余搜索空间表示。

Result: 证明了失败条件更新能正确捕获后续决策的后验置信，确保贪心序列中的每一步都基于轨迹模型固有时间相关性的统计一致表示。推导了相应的贝叶斯更新公式，为不确定性下的规划提供了系统框架。

Conclusion: 该概率框架为自主会合应用中的不确定性规划提供了系统方法，能够处理稀疏噪声观测和快速移动目标，通过序列贪心决策优化会合成功率，适用于多种实际应用场景。

Abstract: We develop a probabilistic framework for \emph{rendezvous planning}: given sparse, noisy observations of a fast-moving target, plan rendezvous spatiotemporal coordinates for a set of significantly slower seeking agents. The unknown target trajectory is estimated under uncertain dynamics using a filtering approach that combines a kernel-based maximum a posteriori estimation with Gaussian process correction, producing a mixture over trajectory hypotheses. This estimate is used to select spatiotemporal rendezvous points that maximize the probability of successful rendezvous. Points are chosen sequentially by greedily minimizing failure probability in the current belief space, which is updated after each step by conditioning on unsuccessful rendezvous attempts. We show that the failure-conditioned update correctly captures the posterior belief for subsequent decisions, ensuring that each step in the greedy sequence is informed by a statistically consistent representation of the remaining search space, and derive the corresponding Bayesian updates incorporating temporal correlations intrinsic to the trajectory model. This result provides a systematic framework for planning under uncertainty in applications of autonomous rendezvous such as unmanned aerial vehicle refueling, spacecraft servicing, autonomous surface vessel operations, search and rescue missions, and missile defense. In each, the motion of the target entity can be modeled using a system of differential equations undergoing optimal control for a chosen objective, in our example case Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman solutions for minimum arrival time of a Dubins car with uncertain turning radius and destination.

</details>


### [120] [Discrete-Time Event-Triggered Extremum Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01450)
*Victor Hugo Pereira Rodrigues,Tiago Roux Oliveira,Miroslav Krstić,Frank Allgöwer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出离散时间事件触发极值搜索控制方案，用于非线性系统的实时优化，仅在满足触发条件时更新控制输入，减少不必要的执行和通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离散时间极值搜索控制依赖周期性更新，导致不必要的执行器动作和通信开销。需要开发资源感知的实时优化方法，在保持优化能力的同时减少输入更新频率。

Method: 结合极值搜索与事件触发机制，仅当状态相关的触发条件满足时才更新控制输入。使用离散时间平均法和李雅普诺夫分析，证明轨迹在未知极值点邻域内的实际收敛性，以及相关平均动态的指数稳定性。

Result: 该方法在保持经典极值搜索优化能力的同时，显著减少了输入更新次数。仿真结果验证了该方案在资源感知实时优化中的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的离散时间事件触发极值搜索控制方案为非线性系统实时优化提供了资源高效的解决方案，通过自适应确定输入更新时刻，在保证收敛性的同时减少了执行和通信开销。

Abstract: This paper proposes a discrete-time event-triggered extremum seeking control scheme for real-time optimization of nonlinear systems. Unlike conventional discrete-time implementations relying on periodic updates, the proposed approach updates the control input only when a state-dependent triggering condition is satisfied, reducing unnecessary actuation and communication. The resulting closed-loop system combines extremum seeking with an event-triggering mechanism that adaptively determines the input update instants. Using discrete-time averaging and Lyapunov analysis, we establish practical convergence of the trajectories to a neighborhood of the unknown extremum point and show exponential stability of the associated average dynamics. The proposed method preserves the optimization capability of classical extremum seeking while significantly reducing the number of input updates. Simulation results illustrate the effectiveness of the approach for resource-aware real-time optimization.

</details>


### [121] [On Integral Linear Constraints on Convex Cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01511)
*Emil Vladu,Alexandre Megretski,Anders Rantzer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文研究了积分线性约束和耗散不等式在凸锥约束下的充分必要条件，建立了与线性二次控制理论的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在凸锥约束下的积分线性约束和耗散不等式，建立与线性矩阵不等式类似的理论框架，并将其应用于线性二次控制中的经典结果。

Method: 考虑有限维赋范向量空间中点态受限于凸锥的轨迹集合，证明积分线性约束和耗散不等式成立的充分必要条件是存在满足锥不等式的有界线性泛函。

Result: 建立了积分线性约束与有界线性泛函存在性之间的等价关系，通过适当选择锥结构，可以推导出正系统理论中的L1增益有界实引理类比，以及线性二次控制中Kalman-Yakubovich-Popov引理的非严格版本。

Conclusion: 论文为凸锥约束下的积分线性约束和耗散不等式提供了统一的理论框架，建立了与线性矩阵不等式理论的类比，并将结果应用于线性二次控制中的经典引理，扩展了现有理论的应用范围。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider integral linear constraints and the dissipation inequality with linear supply rates for certain sets of trajectories confined pointwise in time to a convex cone which belongs to a finite-dimensional normed vector space. Such constraints are then shown to be satisfied if and only if a bounded linear functional exists which satisfies a conic inequality. This is analogous to the typical situation in which a quadratic supply rate over the entire space is related to a linear matrix inequality. A connection is subsequently drawn precisely to linear-quadratic control: by proper choice of cone, the main results can be applied to produce a known L1-gain analogue to the bounded real lemma in positive systems theory, as well as a non-strict version of the Kalman-Yakubovich-Popov Lemma in linear-quadratic control.

</details>


### [122] [Strong Convergence of FISTA for Affinely Constrained Convex Quadratic Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01636)
*Sedi Bartz,Heinz H. Bauschke,Yuan Gao,Walaa M. Moursi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在仿射二次情况下FISTA的强收敛性，无需闭性假设，并推广到无约束凸二次目标函数时得到Nesterov加速梯度法的强收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决FISTA强收敛性的开放问题：虽然已有研究证明了FISTA的弱收敛性，但强收敛性仅在特定条件下成立。本文旨在消除对闭性假设的依赖，在更一般的仿射二次情况下建立强收敛性。

Method: 针对仿射二次优化问题，分析FISTA算法的收敛行为。通过数学分析证明，在无需闭性假设的条件下，算法产生的序列强收敛到最优解。特别地，将结果应用于无约束凸二次目标函数，得到Nesterov加速梯度法的强收敛性。

Result: 证明了在仿射二次情况下FISTA的强收敛性，无需任何闭性假设。作为特例，对于无约束凸二次目标函数，Nesterov加速梯度法具有强收敛性。

Conclusion: 本文解决了FISTA强收敛性的重要理论问题，在仿射二次情况下建立了无需闭性假设的强收敛结果，为加速梯度方法提供了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: In October 2025, research by Boţ, Fadili, and Nguyen, and by Jang and Ryu, led to the seminal result that Beck and Teboulle's FISTA converges weakly to a minimizer of the sum of two convex functions resolving a long-standing open problem. The first strong convergence result was obtained in November 2025 by Moursi, Naguib, Pavlovic, and Vavasis for affinely constrained convex minimization provided certain closedness conditions hold.
  In this paper, we prove strong convergence in the affine-quadratic case without any closedness assumption. Specializing this to the unconstrained case, we obtain the strong convergence of Nesterov's accelerated gradient method when applied to a convex quadratic objective function.

</details>


### [123] [Day-Ahead Offering for Virtual Power Plants: A Stochastic Linear Programming Reformulation and Projected Subgradient Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01755)
*Weiqi Meng,Hongyi Li,Bai Cui*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于内近似的投影次梯度方法，用于解决虚拟电厂在日前市场中的报价问题，相比传统列约束生成算法实现了约两个数量级的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟电厂作为聚合分布式能源资源参与电力系统协调运行的新兴范式，面临着日前报价的关键运营挑战。该问题需要在高度间歇性和不确定的分布式能源输出及市场价格条件下，确定价格-数量对，平衡利润机会与运营不确定性。

Method: 首先将问题建模为基于场景的两阶段随机自适应鲁棒优化问题，其中节点边际价格不确定性遵循马尔可夫过程，分布式能源不确定性由静态不确定性集表征。然后基于列约束生成算法的外近似原理，提出了一种新颖的内近似投影次梯度方法。通过利用问题结构，提出了两种改进计算可处理性的方法：1) 在温和建模假设下，将鲁棒第二阶段问题等价重构为具有嵌套资源分配结构的线性规划；2) 利用重构后的第二阶段原问题的计算效率和第一阶段可行域的保序结构，提出高效的投影次梯度算法求解整体随机线性规划问题。

Result: 使用真实世界数据进行的大量计算实验表明，整体投影次梯度下降方法相比列约束生成算法实现了约两个数量级的加速，同时保持了解决方案质量。

Conclusion: 本文提出的内近似投影次梯度方法为虚拟电厂的日前报价问题提供了高效的计算解决方案，显著提升了计算效率，同时保持了优化质量，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Virtual power plants (VPPs) are an emerging paradigm that aggregates distributed energy resources (DERs) for coordinated participation in power systems, including bidding as a single dispatchable entity in the wholesale market. In this paper, we address a critical operational challenge for VPPs: the day-ahead offering problem under highly intermittent and uncertain DER outputs and market prices. The day-ahead offering problem determines the price-quantity pairs submitted by VPPs while balancing profit opportunities against operational uncertainties. First, we formulate the problem as a scenario-based two-stage stochastic adaptive robust optimization problem, where the uncertainty of the locational marginal prices follows a Markov process and DER uncertainty is characterized by static uncertainty sets. Then, motivated by the outer approximation principle of the column-and-constraint generation (CC&G) algorithm, we propose a novel inner approximation-based projected subgradient method. By exploiting the problem structure, we propose two novel approaches to improve computational tractability. First, we show that under mild modeling assumptions, the robust second-stage problem can be equivalently reformulated as a linear program (LP) with a nested resource allocation structure that is amenable to an efficient greedy algorithm. Furthermore, motivated by the computational efficiency of solving the reformulated primal second-stage problem and the isotonic structure of the first-stage feasible region, we propose an efficient projected subgradient algorithm to solve the overall stochastic LP problem. Extensive computational experiments using real-world data demonstrate that the overall projected subgradient descent method achieves about two orders of magnitude speedup over CC&G while maintaining solution quality.

</details>


### [124] [State-constrained optimal control of the continuity equation and infinite-dimensional viscosity solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01768)
*Fabio Bagagiolo,Ivan Romanò*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究带加权积分状态约束的连续性方程有限时域最优控制问题，在希尔伯特框架下证明值函数的Lipschitz连续性、动态规划原理及约束HJB方程的粘性解性质，并建立比较原理和唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究连续性方程在固定集外质量加权积分约束下的最优控制问题，旨在建立无限维约束Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程的粘性解理论框架。

Method: 在希尔伯特密度空间框架下，通过构造合适的紧不变子集，证明值函数的Lipschitz连续性，利用动态规划原理推导约束HJB方程，并建立粘性解理论（内部为下解，边界为上解）。

Result: 证明了值函数在紧不变子集上的Lipschitz连续性，建立了动态规划原理，证明了约束HJB方程在粘性解意义下的成立性（内部下解，边界上解），并建立了比较原理和Lipschitz类中的唯一性。

Conclusion: 成功建立了带加权积分状态约束的连续性方程最优控制问题的无限维HJB方程粘性解理论框架，为这类约束控制问题提供了严格的分析基础。

Abstract: We study a finite horizon optimal control problem for the continuity equation under a weighted integral state constraint on the mass outside a fixed set. The model is cast in a Hilbert framework for densities. On a suitable invariant compact subset, we prove that the value function is Lipschitz continuous and satisfies, by dynamic programming, the associated infinite dimensional constrained Hamilton Jacobi Bellman equation in viscosity sense (subsolution in the interior, supersolution up to the boundary). We finally prove a comparison principle and uniqueness in the Lipschitz class.

</details>


### [125] [Improving Operational Feasibility in Large-Scale Power System Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01771)
*Gereon Recht,Oussama Alaya,Benedikt Jahn,Karl-Kiên Cao,Hendrik Lens*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种通过重调度和增强初始解来解决大规模交流容量扩展问题的方法，确保交流可行性并支持后续动态电网仿真


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模电力系统规划通常使用线性化、仅考虑有功功率的潮流方程近似，忽略许多运行约束，并在强简化假设下测试系统运行可行性，需要更全面的方法

Method: 通过重调度和增强初始解获得交流容量扩展问题的解决方案；考虑发电机、无功补偿设备、储能系统和输电的同时扩展；包含启动程序和电源能力曲线的运行约束，以及电压角差和电压幅值的简化稳定性限制；测试多种现有和改进的潮流近似方法，将其集成到迭代输电扩展规划方法中，获得凸化公式

Result: 在大不列颠和爱尔兰岛屿的输电级大规模问题实例上验证方法；发现包含输电损耗对确定初始解最为关键，可减少获得交流可行解所需的重调度和增强量；而包含无功约束未带来进一步改进

Conclusion: 该方法在弱假设下确保交流可行系统，保证稳态电压稳定性，并允许后续动态电网仿真，对于规划未来逆变器主导的稳定电力系统至关重要

Abstract: Large-scale power system planning mostly uses linearized, active power only approximations of the power flow equations, ignores many operational constraints, and tests the operational feasibility of the resulting systems only under strongly simplifying assumptions. We propose an approach to obtain solutions to large instances of the alternating current capacity expansion problem via redispatch and reinforcement of an initial solution. The problem formulation considers simultaneous expansion of generators, reactive compensation devices, storage systems, and transmission. Furthermore, it includes operational constraints via startup procedures and capability curves of power sources and simplified stability limits via constraints on voltage angle differences and voltage magnitudes. To obtain initial solutions, we test several established and partly modified power flow approximations and integrate them into an approach for iterative transmission expansion planning, thereby obtaining convex formulations. We demonstrate the approach on large problem instances covering the islands of Great Britain and Ireland at the transmission level, for which we extend the open data source to model reactive power. We find that including transmission losses to determine the initial solution is most decisive, as the amount of redispatch and reinforcements necessary to obtain an alternating current feasible solution is reduced, whereas incorporating reactive power constraints did not lead to further improvements. Our approach ensures an alternating current feasible system under weak assumptions, thus guaranteeing steady-state voltage stability and allowing subsequent dynamic grid simulations, which is instrumental for planning stable future inverter-dominated power systems.

</details>


### [126] [Stability of the Timoshenko Beam Equation with One Weakly Degenerate Local Kelvin-Voigt Damping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01809)
*Ruijuan Liu,Qiong Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究具有局部分布Kelvin-Voigt阻尼的Timoshenko梁方程，该阻尼系数存在奇异性导致导数不连续，证明了系统具有多项式稳定性，衰减率为t^{-1/2}


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Timoshenko梁方程在局部Kelvin-Voigt阻尼作用下的稳定性问题，特别关注阻尼系数存在奇异性（导数不连续）的情况，分析阻尼作用于剪切应力或弯曲力矩时系统的衰减行为

Method: 使用频域方法和乘子技术，分析阻尼系数存在奇异性的Timoshenko梁方程，考虑局部Kelvin-Voigt阻尼作用于剪切应力或弯曲力矩两种情况

Result: 证明了无论局部Kelvin-Voigt阻尼作用于剪切应力还是弯曲力矩，关联的半群都具有多项式稳定性，系统以速率t^{-1/2}衰减

Conclusion: 具有局部分布Kelvin-Voigt阻尼的Timoshenko梁系统，即使阻尼系数存在奇异性导致导数不连续，系统仍能保持多项式稳定性，衰减率为t^{-1/2}，这一结果适用于阻尼作用于剪切应力或弯曲力矩两种情况

Abstract: We consider the Timoshenko beam equation with locally distributed Kelvin-Voigt damping, which affects either the shear stress or the bending moment. The damping coefficient exhibits a singularity, causing its derivative to be discontinuous. By using the frequency domain method and multiplier technique, we prove that the associated semigroup is polynomial stability. Specifically, regardless of whether the local Kelvin-Voigt damping acts on the shear stress or the bending moment, the system decays polynomially with rate $t^{-\frac{1}{2}}$.

</details>


### [127] [Hausdorff compactness and regularity for classes of open sets under geometric constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01813)
*Mohamed Barkatou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文在ℝ²和ℝ³中引入了一类具有向内法向量几何特性的新型开集，证明了这些集合在Hausdorff拓扑下的紧致性，建立了Hausdorff收敛、紧收敛和特征函数收敛的等价性，并研究了厚度函数的正则性以及凸集C对可容许形状边界正则性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有特定几何性质的开集类，这些集合通过向内法向量特性定义，旨在建立这些集合在Hausdorff拓扑下的紧致性理论，为形状优化和几何分析提供理论基础。

Method: 引入基于向内法向量几何特性的新型开集类，运用Hausdorff拓扑理论分析集合收敛性，建立不同收敛概念（Hausdorff、紧收敛、特征函数收敛）之间的等价关系，研究厚度函数的正则性，并分析固定凸集C对可容许形状边界正则性的影响。

Result: 证明了所引入开集类在Hausdorff拓扑下的紧致性结果；建立了特定类中Hausdorff收敛、紧收敛和特征函数收敛的等价性；获得了厚度函数的正则性结果；揭示了固定凸集C的正则性如何影响可容许形状的边界正则性。

Conclusion: 该研究为具有向内法向量几何特性的开集类建立了系统的紧致性理论，证明了不同收敛概念的等价性，并深入分析了厚度函数和边界正则性，为形状优化和几何分析提供了重要的理论基础。

Abstract: This article introduces innovative classes of open sets in \(\mathbb{R}^{N}\), where \(N=2, 3\), characterized by a geometric property associated with the inward normal. The focus lies on proving compactness results for the Hausdorff topology within these classes. Furthermore, the paper establishes the equivalence of convergences, encompassing Hausdorff, compact, and characteristic functions, for select classes. We also investigate the regularity of the thickness function associated with these domains and analyze how the regularity of the fixed convex set \(C\) influences the boundary regularity of the admissible shapes.

</details>


### [128] [Bilevel Programming Approach for Image Restoration Problems with Automatically Hyperparameter Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01866)
*Hang Xie,Xuewen Li,Peili Li,Qiuyu Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种双层规划方法来自动选择图像恢复中的超参数，通过可行性惩罚函数和近端点项解决非光滑凸规划问题，证明算法收敛到KKT稳定点，实验显示恢复质量更好且计算时间更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于优化的图像恢复模型中，超参数的正确选择对获得优异性能至关重要。然而，当前研究通常涉及手动调整这些超参数，这非常耗时且往往不够准确。因此需要开发自动选择超参数的方法。

Method: 提出一种双层规划方法，能够同时选择最优超参数并实现高质量恢复结果。将双层规划问题重新表述为包含凸差函数不等式约束的形式，然后通过可行性惩罚函数和近端点项解决一系列非光滑凸规划问题。下层问题通过交替方向乘子法求解。

Result: 理论上证明算法生成的序列收敛到不等式约束等价双层规划问题的Karush-Kuhn-Tucker稳定点。在仿真和真实图像上进行测试，结果表明所提算法在恢复质量上优于其他超参数选择方法，同时需要更少的计算时间。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种有效的自动超参数选择方法，通过双层规划框架同时优化超参数和恢复结果，在理论和实验上都验证了其优越性，为图像恢复中的超参数选择问题提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: In optimization-based image restoration models, the correct selection of hyperparameters is crucial for achieving superior performance. However, current research typically involves manual tuning of these hyperparameters, which is highly time-consuming and often lacks accuracy. In this paper, we concentrate on the automated selection of hyperparameters in the context of image restoration and present a bilevel programming approach that can simultaneously select the optimal hyperparameters and achieve high-quality restoration results. For implementation, we reformulate the bilevel programming problem that incorporates an inequality constraint related to the difference-of-convex functions. Following this, we address a sequence of nonsmooth convex programming problems by employing a feasibility penalty function along with a proximal point term. In this context, the nonsmooth convex programming problem uses the solution of the lower-level problem, which is derived through the alternating direction method of multipliers. Theoretically, we prove that the sequence generated by the algorithm converges to a Karush-Kuhn-Tucker stationary point of the inequality-constrained equivalent bilevel programming problem. We conduct a series of tests on both simulations and real images, which demonstrate that the proposed algorithm achieve superior restoration quality while requiring less computing time compared to other hyperparameter selection methods.

</details>


### [129] [Scaled Relative Graphs and Dynamic Integral Quadratic Constraints: Connections and Computations for Nonlinear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01873)
*Timo de Groot,Tom Oomen,W. P. M. H. Heemels,Sebastiaan van den Eijnden*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于动态积分二次约束的系统方法，用于计算非线性系统的软硬缩放相对图，通过S-过程获得可处理的SRG上界，并用于Lur'e型系统的反馈稳定性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缩放相对图（SRGs）为非线性系统提供了图形化分析和设计工具，但需要系统化的计算方法。本文旨在开发一种基于动态积分二次约束的框架，以计算软硬SRGs，并建立与经典IQC稳定性结果的图形化联系。

Method: 采用动态积分二次约束（IQCs）和S-过程来获得可处理的SRG上界。通过IQC乘子定义复平面区域：软SRG通过频域条件计算，硬SRG通过乘子的硬分解和线性矩阵不等式获得。

Result: 开发了系统化的软硬SRG计算方法，建立了SRG上界与动态乘子IQC稳定性结果之间的图形化联系，为Lur'e型系统提供了基于SRG的反馈稳定性判据。

Conclusion: 提出的动态IQC框架为非线性系统的SRG计算提供了系统化方法，将经典IQC稳定性理论转化为图形化解释，扩展了缩放相对图在非线性系统分析和设计中的应用。

Abstract: Scaled relative graphs (SRGs) enable graphical analysis and design of nonlinear systems. In this paper, we present a systematic approach for computing both soft and hard SRGs of nonlinear systems using dynamic integral quadratic constraints (IQCs). These constraints are exploited via application of the S-procedure to compute tractable SRG overbounds. In particular, we show that the multipliers associated with the IQCs define regions in the complex plane. Soft SRG computations are formulated through frequency-domain conditions, while hard SRGs are obtained via hard factorizations of multipliers and linear matrix inequalities. The overbounds are used to derive an SRG-based feedback stability result for Lur'e-type systems, providing a new graphical interpretation of classical IQC stability results with dynamic multipliers.

</details>


### [130] [When does learning pay off? A study on DRL-based dynamic algorithm configuration for carbon-aware scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01886)
*Andrea Mencaroni,Robbert Reijnen,Yingqian Zhang,Dieter Claeys*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: DRL用于动态算法配置可学习泛化控制策略，在训练实例分布外仍有效，相比静态调优在复杂场景中表现更优


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究DRL在动态算法配置中的泛化能力，验证训练成本是否值得投资，特别是在真实优化问题中

Method: 开发基于DRL的DAC框架，仅在小规模简单实例上训练，然后在相似和复杂未见实例上部署学习策略

Result: DRL学习策略可有效迁移到不同未见实例；在类似训练实例上表现与静态调优相当，在复杂场景中持续优于静态调优

Conclusion: DRL可学习鲁棒且可泛化的控制策略，初始计算投资在静态调优难以适应变化场景时特别有价值

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has recently emerged as a promising tool for Dynamic Algorithm Configuration (DAC), enabling evolutionary algorithms to adapt their parameters online rather than relying on static tuned configurations. While DRL can learn effective control policies, training is computationally expensive. This cost may be justified if learned policies generalize, allowing the training effort to transfer across instance types and problem scales. Yet, for real-world optimization problems, it remains unclear whether this promise holds in practice and under which conditions the investment in learning pays off. In this work, we investigate this question in the context of the carbon-aware permutation flow-shop scheduling problem. We develop a DRL-based DAC framework and train it exclusively on small, simple instances. We then deploy the learned policy on both similar and more complex unseen instances and compare its performance against a static tuned baseline, which provides a fair point of comparison. Our findings show that the proposed method provides a strong dynamic algorithm control policy that can be effectively transferred to different unseen problem instances. Notably, on simple and cheap to compute instances, similar to those observed during training and tuning, DRL performs comparably with the statically tuned baseline. However, as instance characteristics diverge and computational complexities increase, the DRL-learned policy continuously outperforms static tuning. These results confirm that DRL can acquire robust and generalizable control policies which are effective beyond the training instance distributions. This ability to generalize across instance types makes the initial computational investment worthwhile, particularly in settings where static tuning struggles to adapt to changing problem scenarios.

</details>


### [131] [Output Corridor Impulsive Control of First-order Continuous System with Non-local Attractivity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01959)
*Alexander Medvedev,Anton V. Proskurnikov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 设计用于连续标量时不变线性系统的脉冲控制器，适用于化学动力学模型，通过离散剂量和间隔控制实现输出在预定走廊内的稳定周期性运动


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决过程控制和药代动力学中离散剂量应用的控制问题，其中剂量和给药间隔都需要调控，系统具有连续过程和离散控制的混合动力学特性

Method: 设计稳定的周期性解（1-循环），使其一维轨道与预定输出走廊重合，建立确保1-循环局部和全局吸引性的条件

Result: 提出了脉冲控制器设计方法，能够将系统输出限制在预定走廊内，并以静脉注射对乙酰氨基酚的剂量问题作为数值示例验证

Conclusion: 该方法为具有混合动力学的离散剂量控制系统提供了一种有效的设计框架，特别适用于工程和医学中的脉冲控制应用

Abstract: This paper addresses the design of an impulsive controller for a continuous scalar time-invariant linear plant that constitutes the simplest conceivable model of chemical kinetics. The model is ubiquitous in process control as well as pharmacometrics and readily generalizes to systems of Wiener structure. Given the impulsive nature of the feedback, the control problem formulation is particularly suited to discrete dosing applications in engineering and medicine, where both doses and inter-dose intervals are manipulated. Since the feedback controller acts at discrete time instants and employs both amplitude and frequency modulation, whereas the plant is continuous, the closed-loop system exhibits hybrid dynamics featuring complex nonlinear phenomena. The problem of confining the plant output to a predefined corridor of values is considered. The method at the heart of the proposed approach is to design a stable periodic solution, called a 1-cycle, whose one-dimensional orbit coincides with the predefined corridor. Conditions ensuring local and global attractivity of the 1-cycle are established. As a numerical illustration of the proposed approach, the problem of intravenous paracetamol dosing is considered.

</details>


### [132] [Balancing Morality and Economics: Population Games with Herding and Inertia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02030)
*Raghupati Vyas,Harsitha Devaraj,Veeraruna Kavitha*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究清洁技术采用的多类型平均场博弈模型，分析理性、从众和惰性三类消费者的技术选择行为，发现人口构成对清洁技术普及的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究清洁技术采用在减少二氧化碳排放中的重要作用，特别关注具有异质行为倾向的大规模消费者群体中的技术采用动态。

Method: 建立多类型平均场博弈模型，将消费者分为理性型（权衡价格和道德激励）、从众型（跟随多数选择）和惰性型（对新技术有惯性）。使用α-理性纳什均衡及其多类型扩展来表征均衡清洁技术采用水平，并通过随机轮换行为动态的极限识别稳定子集。

Result: 人口构成对清洁技术采用水平起决定性作用。广泛采用需要清洁技术价格劣势足够小，或者存在足够大的从众型人口群体，可通过社会意识项目影响。环境损害本身不足以激励清洁技术采用增加。

Conclusion: 清洁技术普及的关键在于人口行为构成和价格因素，而非环境损害激励。政策应关注价格补贴和利用从众效应通过社会意识项目推动技术采用。

Abstract: The adoption of clean technologies (CTs) plays an important role in reducing carbon dioxide (CO$_2$) emissions. We study CT adoption in a large population of consumers with heterogeneous behavioral tendencies. We model the interaction among the agents as a multi-type mean-field game in which the agents choose between clean and polluting technology based products and may either behave as rationals (trading off price and moral incentives), herding agents (just follow the majority), or lethargic agents exhibiting inertia toward adopting the new technologies. We characterize equilibrium CT adoption levels using the recently introduced notion of $\boldsymbolα$-Rational Nash Equilibrium ($\boldsymbolα$-RNE) and its multi-type extension. We then identify a stable subset using the limits of a stochastic turn-by-turn behavioral dynamics. Our results highlight the role of population composition in determining CT adoption. In particular, widespread adoption requires either a sufficiently small price disadvantage for CTs or the presence of a sufficiently large herding population that can be influenced through social awareness programs. Surprisingly, we could prove that environmental damages do not provide sufficient incentives to increase CT adoption.

</details>


### [133] [Faster Symmetric Rendezvous on Four or More Locations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02058)
*Javier Cembrano,Felix Fischer,Max Klimm*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 对称约会问题中，Anderson-Weber策略对于n≥4不是最优的，存在更优策略


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对称约会问题中Anderson-Weber策略的最优性，该策略已知对n=2,3最优，但对n=4有证据表明不是最优

Method: 构造了一个新的策略，该策略比Anderson-Weber策略具有更小的期望相遇时间

Result: 证明了对于所有n≥4，Anderson-Weber策略都不是最优的

Conclusion: 对称约会问题中Anderson-Weber策略的最优性仅限于n=2,3，对于更大的n存在更优策略

Abstract: In the symmetric rendezvous problem two players follow the same (randomized) strategy to visit one of $n$ locations in each time step $t=0,1,2,\dots$. Their goal is to minimize the expected time until they visit the same location and thus meet. Anderson and Weber [J. Appl. Prob., 1990] proposed a strategy that operates in rounds of $n-1$ steps: a player either remains in one location for $n-1$ steps or visits the other $n-1$ locations in random order; the choice between these two options is made with a probability that depends only on $n$. The strategy is known to be optimal for $n=2$ and $n=3$, and there is convincing evidence that it is not optimal for $n=4$. We show that it is not optimal for any $n\geq 4$, by constructing a strategy with a smaller expected meeting time.

</details>


### [134] [Fixed-time-stable ODE Representation of Lasso](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02069)
*Liang Wu,Yunhong Che,Wallace Gian Yion Tan,Efstathios Iliakis,Richard D. Braatz,Ján Drgoňa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种固定时间稳定的ODE表示方法来解决Lasso问题，通过将其转化为光滑非负二次规划，并设计无投影的牛顿型固定时间稳定ODE系统来求解KKT条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lasso问题在信号处理、机器学习和控制等领域广泛存在，并与神经科学中观察到的稀疏编码机制密切相关。连续时间ODE表示不仅能在模拟计算机上求解Lasso问题，还为解释神经生理现象提供了框架。

Method: 首先将Lasso问题转化为光滑的非负二次规划(QP)，然后设计无投影的牛顿型固定时间稳定ODE系统来求解相应的KKT条件。该ODE的稳定时间与问题数据无关且可任意设定。

Result: 数值实验验证了轨迹在预设时间内达到最优解。ODE的稳定时间独立于问题数据，可以任意规定。

Conclusion: 提出的固定时间稳定ODE表示方法为Lasso问题提供了一种有效的连续时间求解框架，具有预设收敛时间的优势，适用于模拟计算和神经科学解释。

Abstract: Lasso problems arise in many areas, including signal processing, machine learning, and control, and are closely connected to sparse coding mechanisms observed in neuroscience. A continuous-time ordinary differential equation (ODE) representation of the Lasso problem not only enables its solution on analog computers but also provides a framework for interpreting neurophysiological phenomena. This article proposes a fixed-time-stable ODE representation of the Lasso problem by first transforming it into a smooth nonnegative quadratic program (QP) and then designing a projection-free Newton-based ODE representation of the Lasso problem by first transforming it into a smooth nonnegative quadratic program (QP) and then designing a projection-free Newton-based fixed-time-stable ODE system for solving the corresponding Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) conditions. Moreover, the settling time of the ODE is independent of the problem data and can be arbitrarily prescribed. Numerical experiments verify that the trajectory reaches the optimal solution within the prescribed time.

</details>


### [135] [Safe Control of Feedback-Interconnected Systems via Singular Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02132)
*Stefano Di Gregorio,Guido Carnevale,Giuseppe Notarstefano*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于奇异摄动理论的方法，将降阶模型的安全证书扩展到具有不同时间尺度的反馈互联系统，通过设计复合控制障碍函数来保证整个系统的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代复杂系统通常涉及不同时间尺度子系统的反馈互联，安全约束往往只涉及系统的一部分。传统控制障碍函数方法在处理这类多时间尺度系统时面临挑战，需要开发能够将降阶模型的安全证书扩展到整个互联系统的正式方法。

Method: 受奇异摄动稳定性分析的启发，开发了一种正式程序，将降阶模型设计的安全证书提升到整体反馈互联系统。在慢动态和快动态之间存在足够时间尺度分离的条件下，设计复合控制障碍函数来证明互联系统安全集的前向不变性。在线安全滤波器只需为低维降阶模型求解。

Result: 在两种场景下进行了数值测试：(1) 具有关节电机动力学的机械臂系统；(2) 由优化算法驱动的物理设备。结果表明所提方法能够有效保证多时间尺度互联系统的安全性，同时减少在线计算负担。

Conclusion: 该方法成功地将奇异摄动理论扩展到安全分析领域，为具有不同时间尺度的反馈互联系统提供了一种系统化的安全证书设计框架，显著降低了在线安全滤波的计算复杂度。

Abstract: Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) have emerged as a powerful tool in the design of safety-critical controllers for nonlinear systems. In modern applications, complex systems often involve the feedback interconnection of subsystems evolving at different timescales, e.g., two parts from different physical domains (e.g., the electrical and mechanical parts of robotic systems) or a physical plant and an (optimization or control) algorithm. In these scenarios, safety constraints often involve only a portion of the overall system. Inspired by singular perturbations for stability analysis, we develop a formal procedure to lift a safety certificate designed on a reduced-order model to the overall feedback-interconnected system. Specifically, we show that under a sufficient timescale separation between slow and fast dynamics, a composite CBF can be designed to certify the forward invariance of the safe set for the interconnected system. As a result, the online safety filter only needs to be solved for the lower-dimensional, reduced-order model. We numerically test the proposed approach on: (i) a robotic arm with joint motor dynamics, and (ii) a physical plant driven by an optimization algorithm.

</details>


### [136] [Data-Driven Tube-Based Zonotopic Predictive Control With Nonconvex Layered Terminal Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02159)
*Zhen Zhang,Bogdan Gheorghe,Florin Stoican,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于数据驱动的管状zonotopic预测控制框架，采用非凸分层终端集设计，通过解耦稳定性验证、可行性扩展和运动区域筛选三个组件，提升控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DTZPC方案通常依赖单一椭球终端集，这可能导致保守性并限制可行性。需要一种更灵活的分层终端集设计来解耦不同控制目标，避免高维DTZPC中的概率反馈综合，同时减少区间矩阵松弛和逆运算的保守性。

Method: 1. 离线设计反馈增益与收缩约束zonotope作为稳定性验证的终端成分；2. 推导逆容许闭环模型集的数据驱动表征，避免区间矩阵松弛和逆运算的保守性；3. 结合精确集合乘法，获得固定闭环动态下最大鲁棒正不变集的内外近似；4. 内近似作为非凸终端集扩展可行性，外近似提供认证的运动区域描述用于快速筛选和监控。

Result: 数值实验表明，相比现有凸终端DTZPC方案，该方法实现了更紧的逆集合包络和改善的可行性。分层设计有效解耦了稳定性验证、可行性扩展和运动区域筛选功能。

Conclusion: 提出的DTZPC框架通过非凸分层终端集设计，成功解决了现有方案的保守性和可行性限制问题。该方法避免了高维DTZPC中的概率反馈综合，减少了区间矩阵松弛的保守性，并通过精确集合乘法获得了更好的集合近似，为数据驱动预测控制提供了更灵活有效的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a data-driven tube-based zonotopic predictive control (DTZPC) framework with nonconvex layered terminal sets. Existing DTZPC schemes with closed-loop guarantees typically rely on a single ellipsoidal terminal set, which can be conservative and thereby limit feasibility. We propose a layered terminal-set design that decouples stability certification, feasibility enlargement, and motion-region screening into three components with distinct roles. First, an offline-designed feedback gain together with a contractive constrained zonotope provides a terminal ingredient for stability certification, while avoiding probabilistic feedback synthesis in high-dimensional DTZPC. Second, we derive a data-driven characterization of the inverse admissible closed-loop model set, avoiding the conservatism of interval-matrix relaxation and inversion. Combined with exact set multiplication, this yields inner and outer approximations of the maximal robust positively invariant (MRPI) set under fixed closed-loop dynamics. The inner approximation serves as a nonconvex terminal set to enlarge feasibility, whereas the outer approximation provides certified motion-region descriptions for fast screening and monitoring. Numerical examples demonstrate tighter inverse-set enclosures and improved feasibility over existing convex-terminal DTZPC schemes.

</details>


### [137] [Explicit Distributed MPC: Reducing Computation and Communication Load by Exploiting Facet Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02177)
*Parth R. Brahmbhatt,Hari S. Ganesh,Styliani Avraamidou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出FACET-DiMPC方法，通过基于面片的关键区域探索技术，显著降低迭代自由分布式模型预测控制的计算复杂度，实现实时控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分布式模型预测控制需要多次迭代才能收敛，导致计算和通信负担较重。需要改进迭代自由的分布式MPC方法，以最小化计算量和通信负载。

Method: 基于多参数规划为每个子系统离线计算显式控制律，实现无需子系统间迭代数据交换的实时控制。引入基于面片的关键区域探索技术(FACET)，利用面片特性进行有针对性的关键区域探索，进一步降低计算复杂度。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法实现了与集中式方法相当的控制性能，同时显著减少了通信开销和计算时间。相比经典迭代DiMPC方法平均计算时间减少98%，相比迭代自由DiMPC方法减少42%。

Conclusion: FACET-DiMPC方法在保持控制性能的同时，大幅提升了计算效率，特别适用于具有严格延迟和计算约束的实时控制应用。

Abstract: Classical Distributed Model Predictive Control (DiMPC) requires multiple iterations to achieve convergence, leading to high computational and communication burdens. This work focuses on the improvement of an iteration-free distributed MPC methodology that minimizes computational effort and communication load. The aforementioned methodology leverages multiparametric programming to compute explicit control laws offline for each subsystem, enabling real-time control without iterative data exchanges between subsystems. Extending our previous work on iteration-free DiMPC, here we introduce a FAcet-based Critical region Exploration Technique for iteration-free DiMPC (FACET-DiMPC) that further reduces computational complexity by leveraging facet properties to do targeted critical region exploration. Simulation results demonstrate that the developed method achieves comparable control performance to centralized methods, while significantly reducing communication overhead and computation time. In particular, the proposed methodology offers substantial efficiency gains in terms of the average computation time reduction of 98% compared to classic iterative DiMPC methods and 42% compared to iteration-free DiMPC methods, making it well-suited for real-time control applications with tight latency and computation constraints.

</details>


### [138] [Random-Subspace Sequential Quadratic Programming for Constrained Zeroth-Order Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02202)
*Runyu Zhang,Gioele Zardini*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种零阶随机子空间序列二次规划方法(ZO-RS-SQP)，用于解决仅能通过函数评估获取信息的非线性约束优化问题，通过低维子空间采样和两点方向估计显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零阶方法在高维空间中依赖噪声梯度和雅可比矩阵估计，难以同时实现计算效率和精确约束满足。需要开发一种既能保持SQP结构化约束处理能力，又能降低计算成本的方法。

Method: 提出ZO-RS-SQP方法：1) 每轮迭代采样随机低维子空间；2) 使用两点评估估计投影目标梯度和约束雅可比矩阵；3) 求解降维二次规划计算步长；4) 提供Armijo线搜索变体增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在标准光滑性和正则性假设下，该方法以高概率收敛到一阶KKT点。数值实验表明，该方法在非线性约束问题上有效，每轮迭代评估成本仅与子空间维度而非环境维度相关。

Conclusion: ZO-RS-SQP成功结合了低维子空间采样和SQP结构化约束处理，为仅能通过函数评估求解的高维约束优化问题提供了计算高效且理论保证的解决方案。

Abstract: We study nonlinear constrained optimization problems in which only function evaluations of the objective and constraints are available. Existing zeroth-order methods rely on noisy gradient and Jacobian surrogates in high dimensions, making it difficult to simultaneously achieve computational efficiency and accurate constraint satisfaction. We propose a zeroth-order random-subspace sequential quadratic programming method (ZO-RS-SQP) that combines two-point directional estimation with low-dimensional SQP updates. At each iteration, the method samples a random low-dimensional subspace, estimates the projected objective gradient and constraint Jacobians using two-point evaluations, and solves a reduced quadratic program to compute the step. As a result, the per-iteration evaluation cost scales with the subspace dimension rather than the ambient dimension, while retaining the structured linearized-constraint treatment of SQP. We also consider an Armijo line-search variant that improves robustness in practice. Under standard smoothness and regularity assumptions, we establish convergence to first-order KKT points with high probability. Numerical experiments illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach on nonlinear constrained problems.

</details>


### [139] [On the Convexity of the Solution Set of Linear Complementarity Problem over Tensor Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02231)
*Sonali Sharma,V. Vetrivel,Jein-Shan Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究张量空间线性互补问题解集的凸性，引入T-列充分张量概念，建立解集凸性的等价条件，推导唯一性和可行性蕴含可解性的充分条件


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究张量空间线性互补问题解集的凸性特性，为这类高阶优化问题提供理论分析基础

Method: 引入T-列充分张量概念，研究其性质及与其他结构张量的关系，建立解集凸性的等价条件

Result: 建立了TLCP解集凸性的等价条件，推导了唯一性和可行性蕴含可解性的充分条件

Conclusion: T-列充分张量是理解TLCP解集凸性的关键概念，相关结果为张量空间线性互补问题的理论分析提供了重要工具

Abstract: This paper investigates the convexity of the solution set of the linear complementarity problems over tensor spaces (TLCPs). We introduce the notion of a $T$-column sufficient tensor and study its properties and relationships with several structured tensors. An equivalent condition for the convexity of the solution set of the $\mathrm{TLCP}$ is established. In addition, sufficient conditions for uniqueness and for feasibility implying solvability are derived.

</details>


### [140] [Analytic Optimal Control for a Class of Driftless x-Flat Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02249)
*Raphael Buchinger,Georg Hartl,Lukas Ecker,Markus Schöberl*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对三状态两输入的无漂移x-平坦非线性系统，提出了一种避免数值求解两点边值问题的显式最优轨迹跟踪控制方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无漂移、x-平坦非线性系统的最优轨迹跟踪问题，旨在避免传统方法中需要数值求解两点边值问题的计算负担

Method: 应用庞特里亚金极大值原理，利用几何特性和权重矩阵间的特定关系，推导出协态量的闭式表达式和两个输入的显式反馈控制律

Result: 获得了bang-singular-bang最优控制结构，在奇异弧上跟踪误差动力学简化为二阶线性动力学，避免了数值求解两点边值问题

Conclusion: 该方法在转向轴运动学模型上验证了精确的轨迹跟踪性能，为特定非线性系统提供了一种高效的最优控制解决方案

Abstract: This paper studies optimal trajectory-tracking for driftless, x-flat nonlinear systems with three states and two inputs. The tracking problem is formulated in Bolza form with a quadratic cost of the tracking error and its derivative. Applying Pontryagin's maximum principle yields a mixed regular-singular optimal control problem. By exploiting geometric properties and a specific relation between the weighting matrices, a closed-form expression for the costate and an explicit feedback law for both inputs is derived. Thereby, the numerical solution of a two-point boundary-value problem is avoided. The singular input leads to a bang-singular-bang optimal control structure, while on the singular arc, the tracking error dynamics reduces to a linear dynamics of order two. The approach is illustrated for the kinematic model of a steerable axle, demonstrating accurate trajectory-tracking.

</details>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [DySCo: Dynamic Semantic Compression for Effective Long-term Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01261)
*Xiang Ao,Yinyu Tan,Mengru Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DySCo框架通过动态语义压缩提升时间序列预测能力，包含熵引导动态采样、分层频率增强分解和跨尺度交互混合器，作为即插即用模块显著改善主流模型的长时依赖捕捉并降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测中，延长回望窗口理论上能提供更丰富历史信息，但实践中常引入无关噪声和计算冗余，阻碍模型有效捕捉复杂长时依赖关系。

Method: 提出动态语义压缩(DySCo)框架：1)熵引导动态采样(EGDS)机制自主识别保留高熵片段并压缩冗余趋势；2)分层频率增强分解(HFED)分离高频异常与低频模式；3)跨尺度交互混合器(CSIM)动态融合全局上下文与局部表征。

Result: 实验表明DySCo作为通用即插即用模块，显著增强主流模型捕捉长时相关性的能力，同时降低计算成本。

Conclusion: DySCo框架通过动态语义压缩有效解决了时间序列预测中长回望窗口带来的噪声和冗余问题，提升了模型对长时依赖的捕捉能力。

Abstract: Time series forecasting (TSF) is critical across domains such as finance, meteorology, and energy. While extending the lookback window theoretically provides richer historical context, in practice, it often introduces irrelevant noise and computational redundancy, preventing models from effectively capturing complex long-term dependencies. To address these challenges, we propose a Dynamic Semantic Compression (DySCo) framework. Unlike traditional methods that rely on fixed heuristics, DySCo introduces an Entropy-Guided Dynamic Sampling (EGDS) mechanism to autonomously identify and retain high-entropy segments while compressing redundant trends. Furthermore, we incorporate a Hierarchical Frequency-Enhanced Decomposition (HFED) strategy to separate high-frequency anomalies from low-frequency patterns, ensuring that critical details are preserved during sparse sampling. Finally, a Cross-Scale Interaction Mixer(CSIM) is designed to dynamically fuse global contexts with local representations, replacing simple linear aggregation. Experimental results demonstrate that DySCo serves as a universal plug-and-play module, significantly enhancing the ability of mainstream models to capture long-term correlations with reduced computational cost.

</details>


### [142] [Forecasting Supply Chain Disruptions with Foresight Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01298)
*Benjamin Turtel,Paul Wilczewski,Kris Skotheim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 训练LLM进行供应链中断概率预测，显著优于GPT-5等基线，实现校准良好的决策支持信号


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 供应链中断预测面临罕见高影响事件难以从噪声数据中可靠推理的挑战，通用模型需要任务特定适应

Method: 引入端到端框架，使用已实现中断结果作为监督训练LLM生成校准概率预测，无需显式提示即可诱导结构化概率推理

Result: 模型在准确性、校准性和精确度上显著优于GPT-5等强基线，训练诱导出更结构化可靠的概率推理

Conclusion: 为训练领域特定预测模型提供通用路径，生成决策就绪信号，开源评估数据集支持透明度

Abstract: Anticipating supply chain disruptions before they materialize is a core challenge for firms and policymakers alike. A key difficulty is learning to reason reliably about infrequent, high-impact events from noisy and unstructured inputs - a setting where general-purpose models struggle without task-specific adaptation. We introduce an end-to-end framework that trains LLMs to produce calibrated probabilistic forecasts using realized disruption outcomes as supervision. The resulting model substantially outperforms strong baselines - including GPT-5 - on accuracy, calibration, and precision. We also show that training induces more structured and reliable probabilistic reasoning without explicit prompting. These results suggest a general pathway for training domain-specific forecasting models that produce decision-ready signals. To support transparency we open-source the evaluation dataset used in this study.
  Dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/LightningRodLabs/supply-chain-predictions

</details>


### [143] [An Online Machine Learning Multi-resolution Optimization Framework for Energy System Design Limit of Performance Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01308)
*Oluwamayowa O. Amusat,Luka Grbcic,Remi Patureau,M. Jibran S. Zuberi,Dan Gunter,Michael Wetter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于机器学习的多分辨率优化框架，用于工业过程综合能源系统设计，通过自适应调度优化分辨率减少架构与运行性能差距，降低高保真模型评估需求


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业过程综合能源系统设计需要在从架构级尺寸到高保真动态运行的多重保真度上进行优化和验证。然而，不同保真度模型之间的不匹配会掩盖性能损失的来源，并使架构到运行性能差距的量化变得复杂。

Method: 提出在线机器学习加速的多分辨率优化框架：1）首先通过多目标架构优化选择系统配置和组件容量；2）开发ML加速的多分辨率滚动时域最优控制策略，基于预测不确定性自适应调度优化分辨率，并使用精英低保真解热启动高保真求解。

Result: 在1MW工业热负荷的试点能源系统中，多分辨率策略将架构到运行性能差距相对基于规则的控制器减少了42%，同时相对于无ML指导的相同多保真方法减少了34%的高保真模型评估需求。

Conclusion: 该框架使高保真验证变得可行，提供了实际可达到运行性能的上界，实现了更快、更可靠的设计验证。

Abstract: Designing reliable integrated energy systems for industrial processes requires optimization and verification models across multiple fidelities, from architecture-level sizing to high-fidelity dynamic operation. However, model mismatch across fidelities obscures the sources of performance loss and complicates the quantification of architecture-to-operation performance gaps. We propose an online, machine-learning-accelerated multi-resolution optimization framework that estimates an architecture-specific upper bound on achievable performance while minimizing expensive high-fidelity model evaluations. We demonstrate the approach on a pilot energy system supplying a 1 MW industrial heat load. First, we solve a multi-objective architecture optimization to select the system configuration and component capacities. We then develop an machine learning (ML)-accelerated multi-resolution, receding-horizon optimal control strategy that approaches the achievable-performance bound for the specified architecture, given the additional controls and dynamics not captured by the architectural optimization model. The ML-guided controller adaptively schedules the optimization resolution based on predictive uncertainty and warm-starts high-fidelity solves using elite low-fidelity solutions. Our results on the pilot case study show that the proposed multi-resolution strategy reduces the architecture-to-operation performance gap by up to 42% relative to a rule-based controller, while reducing required high-fidelity model evaluations by 34% relative to the same multi-fidelity approach without ML guidance, enabling faster and more reliable design verification. Together, these gains make high-fidelity verification tractable, providing a practical upper bound on achievable operational performance.

</details>


### [144] [JetPrism: diagnosing convergence for generative simulation and inverse problems in nuclear physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01313)
*Zeyu Xia,Tyler Kim,Trevor Reed,Judy Fox,Geoffrey Fox,Adam Szczepaniak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了条件流匹配（CFM）的标准训练损失在物理应用中具有误导性，提出了JetPrism框架和多指标评估协议来确保生成模型与真实数据的统计一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真蒙特卡洛模拟和复杂逆问题（如将模糊的实验观测映射到真实状态）计算成本高昂但对数据分析至关重要。条件流匹配（CFM）提供了加速这些任务的数学稳健方法，但作者发现其标准训练损失在物理应用中具有根本性误导。

Method: 设计了JetPrism——一个可配置的CFM框架，作为评估无条件生成和条件探测器展开的高效生成替代模型。使用合成压力测试和Jefferson Lab运动学数据集（γp → ρ⁰p → π⁺π⁻p），提出包含边际和成对χ²统计量、W₁距离、相关矩阵距离（D_corr）和最近邻距离比（R_NN）的多指标评估协议。

Result: 研究表明，在标准损失收敛后，物理信息指标仍持续显著改善。JetPrism被证明是可靠的生成替代模型，能够确保与真实数据的精确统计一致性，而不会记忆训练集。

Conclusion: 领域特定的评估必须取代通用损失指标。虽然该诊断框架在核物理中演示，但可轻松扩展到参数生成和复杂逆问题，应用于医学成像、天体物理、半导体发现和量化金融等领域。

Abstract: High-fidelity Monte Carlo simulations and complex inverse problems, such as mapping smeared experimental observations to ground-truth states, are computationally intensive yet essential for robust data analysis. Conditional Flow Matching (CFM) offers a mathematically robust approach to accelerating these tasks, but we demonstrate its standard training loss is fundamentally misleading. In rigorous physics applications, CFM loss plateaus prematurely, serving as an unreliable indicator of true convergence and physical fidelity. To investigate this disconnect, we designed JetPrism, a configurable CFM framework acting as an efficient generative surrogate for evaluating unconditional generation and conditional detector unfolding. Using synthetic stress tests and a Jefferson Lab kinematic dataset ($γp \to ρ^0 p \to π^+π^- p$) relevant to the forthcoming Electron-Ion Collider (EIC), we establish that physics-informed metrics continue to improve significantly long after the standard loss converges. Consequently, we propose a multi-metric evaluation protocol incorporating marginal and pairwise $χ^2$ statistics, $W_1$ distances, correlation matrix distances ($D_{\mathrm{corr}}$), and nearest-neighbor distance ratios ($R_{\mathrm{NN}}$). By demonstrating that domain-specific evaluations must supersede generic loss metrics, this work establishes JetPrism as a dependable generative surrogate that ensures precise statistical agreement with ground-truth data without memorizing the training set. While demonstrated in nuclear physics, this diagnostic framework is readily extensible to parameter generation and complex inverse problems across broad domains. Potential applications span medical imaging, astrophysics, semiconductor discovery, and quantitative finance, where high-fidelity simulation, rigorous inversion, and generative reliability are critical.

</details>


### [145] [Detecting Complex Money Laundering Patterns with Incremental and Distributed Graph Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01315)
*Haseeb Tariq,Alen Kaja,Marwan Hassani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReDiRect框架，通过模糊分区和分布式处理解决反洗钱检测中的规模复杂性和高误报率问题，在无监督设置下实现高效检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反洗钱检测方法存在局限性：洗钱者通过模仿正常交易模式逃避检测，现有算法难以处理大规模复杂交易图，基于风险规则的刚性系统产生过多误报信号，导致实际应用效果不佳。

Method: 提出ReDiRect框架（REduce, DIstribute, and RECTify）：1）在无监督设置下将大规模交易图模糊分区为更小、可管理的组件；2）采用分布式处理实现快速计算；3）定义改进的评估指标以更好地捕捉洗钱模式的有效性。

Result: 在Libra真实数据集和IBM Watson合成数据集上的实验表明，ReDiRect框架在效率和实际应用性方面优于现有和最先进技术，实现了更优的性能表现。

Conclusion: ReDiRect框架通过创新的无监督模糊分区和分布式处理方法，有效解决了反洗钱检测中的规模复杂性、高误报率和实际应用挑战，为金融监控系统提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Money launderers take advantage of limitations in existing detection approaches by hiding their financial footprints in a deceitful manner. They manage this by replicating transaction patterns that the monitoring systems cannot easily distinguish. As a result, criminally gained assets are pushed into legitimate financial channels without drawing attention. Algorithms developed to monitor money flows often struggle with scale and complexity. The difficulty of identifying such activities is further intensified by the (persistent) inability of current solutions to control the excessive number of false positive signals produced by rigid, risk-based rules systems. We propose a framework called ReDiRect (REduce, DIstribute, and RECTify), specifically designed to overcome these challenges. The primary contribution of our work is a novel framing of this problem in an unsupervised setting; where a large transaction graph is fuzzily partitioned into smaller, manageable components to enable fast processing in a distributed manner. In addition, we define a refined evaluation metric that better captures the effectiveness of exposed money laundering patterns. Through comprehensive experimentation, we demonstrate that our framework achieves superior performance compared to existing and state-of-the-art techniques, particularly in terms of efficiency and real-world applicability. For validation, we used the real (open source) Libra dataset and the recently released synthetic datasets by IBM Watson. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/mhaseebtariq/redirect.

</details>


### [146] [Efficient and Principled Scientific Discovery through Bayesian Optimization: A Tutorial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01328)
*Zhongwei Yu,Rasul Tutunov,Alexandre Max Maraval,Zikai Xie,Zhenzhi Tan,Jiankang Wang,Zijing Li,Liangliang Xu,Qi Yang,Jun Jiang,Sanzhong Luo,Zhenxiao Guo,Haitham Bou-Ammar,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本教程介绍贝叶斯优化（BO）作为科学发现的概率驱动框架，通过替代模型和采集函数自动化假设-实验-优化循环，应用于催化、材料科学等领域，提供技术扩展和跨学科实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统科学发现依赖直觉式的假设-实验-优化循环，存在资源浪费、设计低效和关键洞察遗漏的问题。需要一种原则性的概率驱动框架来形式化和自动化这一核心科学循环，消除猜测和手动试错。

Method: 贝叶斯优化框架：1) 将科学发现构建为优化问题；2) 使用替代模型（如高斯过程）将经验观察建模为演化假设；3) 采用采集函数指导实验选择，平衡已知知识的利用和未知领域的探索；4) 提供端到端工作流程；5) 涵盖批量实验、异方差性、上下文优化和人机协同等关键技术扩展。

Result: 通过催化、材料科学、有机合成和分子发现等领域的案例研究，展示了贝叶斯优化在现实世界中的有效性。教程为跨学科研究人员提供分层内容，帮助他们设计更高效的实验，加速原则性科学发现。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯优化为科学发现提供了一个原则性的概率驱动框架，能够形式化和自动化传统的假设-实验-优化循环。该教程弥合了BO的AI进展与自然科学实际应用之间的差距，为跨学科研究人员提供了实用的工具和方法，有望显著提高实验效率并加速科学发现进程。

Abstract: Traditional scientific discovery relies on an iterative hypothesise-experiment-refine cycle that has driven progress for centuries, but its intuitive, ad-hoc implementation often wastes resources, yields inefficient designs, and misses critical insights. This tutorial presents Bayesian Optimisation (BO), a principled probability-driven framework that formalises and automates this core scientific cycle. BO uses surrogate models (e.g., Gaussian processes) to model empirical observations as evolving hypotheses, and acquisition functions to guide experiment selection, balancing exploitation of known knowledge and exploration of uncharted domains to eliminate guesswork and manual trial-and-error. We first frame scientific discovery as an optimisation problem, then unpack BO's core components, end-to-end workflows, and real-world efficacy via case studies in catalysis, materials science, organic synthesis, and molecule discovery. We also cover critical technical extensions for scientific applications, including batched experimentation, heteroscedasticity, contextual optimisation, and human-in-the-loop integration. Tailored for a broad audience, this tutorial bridges AI advances in BO with practical natural science applications, offering tiered content to empower cross-disciplinary researchers to design more efficient experiments and accelerate principled scientific discovery.

</details>


### [147] [Model Merging via Data-Free Covariance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01329)
*Marawan Gamal Abdel Hameed,Derek Tam,Pascal Jr Tikeng Notsawo,Colin Raffel,Guillaume Rabusseau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需数据的模型合并方法，通过从差异矩阵直接估计协方差矩阵，消除了对辅助数据的需求，同时降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法存在两个主要问题：1）启发式方法缺乏理论依据；2）基于干扰最小化的优化方法需要数据来估计协方差矩阵，而数据在实际合并场景中可能不可得。需要一种既理论严谨又无需数据的合并方法。

Method: 重新审视干扰最小化框架，发现在特定条件下，可以直接从差异矩阵（模型参数之间的差异）估计协方差矩阵，无需使用原始数据。这种方法将模型合并表述为层级的优化问题，直接最小化任务间的干扰。

Result: 在视觉和语言基准测试中，从8600万参数到70亿参数的模型上验证了该方法，性能优于先前无需数据的最先进合并方法。

Conclusion: 提出了一种理论严谨且无需数据的模型合并方法，通过从差异矩阵直接估计协方差矩阵，既保持了优化方法的理论优势，又获得了启发式方法的实践便利性。

Abstract: Model merging provides a way of cheaply combining individual models to produce a model that inherits each individual's capabilities. While some merging methods can approach the performance of multitask training, they are often heuristically motivated and lack theoretical justification. A principled alternative is to pose model merging as a layer-wise optimization problem that directly minimizes interference between tasks. However, this formulation requires estimating per-layer covariance matrices from data, which may not be available when performing merging. In contrast, many of the heuristically-motivated methods do not require auxiliary data, making them practically advantageous. In this work, we revisit the interference minimization framework and show that, under certain conditions, covariance matrices can be estimated directly from difference matrices, eliminating the need for data while also reducing computational costs. We validate our approach across vision and language benchmarks on models ranging from 86M parameters to 7B parameters, outperforming previous data-free state-of-the-art merging methods

</details>


### [148] [SECURE: Stable Early Collision Understanding via Robust Embeddings in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01337)
*Wenjing Wang,Wenxuan Wang,Songning Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SECURE框架通过定义和强制执行预测空间与特征空间的一致性、稳定性，提升事故预测模型的鲁棒性，在扰动和干净数据上都取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事故预测模型（如CRASH）在面对微小输入扰动时表现出预测和潜在表示的不稳定性，这对安全关键系统的可靠性构成严重风险。

Method: 提出SECURE框架，基于预测空间和潜在特征空间的一致性和稳定性四个关键属性，采用多目标损失函数微调基线模型，最小化与参考模型的差异并惩罚对对抗性扰动的敏感性。

Result: 在DAD和CCD数据集上的实验表明，该方法不仅显著增强了对各种扰动的鲁棒性，还提高了在干净数据上的性能，取得了新的最先进结果。

Conclusion: SECURE框架通过形式化定义和强制执行模型鲁棒性，有效解决了事故预测系统在现实扰动下的可靠性问题，为安全关键应用提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: While deep learning has significantly advanced accident anticipation, the robustness of these safety-critical systems against real-world perturbations remains a major challenge. We reveal that state-of-the-art models like CRASH, despite their high performance, exhibit significant instability in predictions and latent representations when faced with minor input perturbations, posing serious reliability risks. To address this, we introduce SECURE - Stable Early Collision Understanding Robust Embeddings, a framework that formally defines and enforces model robustness. SECURE is founded on four key attributes: consistency and stability in both prediction space and latent feature space. We propose a principled training methodology that fine-tunes a baseline model using a multi-objective loss, which minimizes divergence from a reference model and penalizes sensitivity to adversarial perturbations. Experiments on DAD and CCD datasets demonstrate that our approach not only significantly enhances robustness against various perturbations but also improves performance on clean data, achieving new state-of-the-art results.

</details>


### [149] [Massively Parallel Exact Inference for Hawkes Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01342)
*Ahmer Raza,Hudson Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于线性指数Hawkes过程最大似然估计的并行算法，利用GPU并行前缀扫描将计算复杂度从O(N²)降低到约O(N/P)，可处理数千节点和数千万事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多元Hawkes过程的最大似然估计计算复杂度为O(N²)，而现有的线性指数Hawkes过程O(N)递归算法是顺序执行的，无法充分利用现代GPU的并行计算能力。

Method: 将Hawkes过程强度表示为稀疏转移矩阵的乘积，这些矩阵允许线性时间关联乘法，从而可以通过并行前缀扫描进行计算。该方法实现了大规模并行化算法，并采用批处理方案保持恒定内存使用。

Result: 在模拟和真实数据集上实现了数量级的加速，可扩展到数千个节点和数千万个事件，远超先前工作的规模。提供了开源PyTorch库实现。

Conclusion: 该方法为线性指数Hawkes过程提供了精确、高效且可扩展的最大似然估计解决方案，保持了模型的简单性和可解释性，同时充分利用了现代GPU的并行计算能力。

Abstract: Multivariate Hawkes processes are a widely used class of self-exciting point processes, but maximum likelihood estimation naively scales as $O(N^2)$ in the number of events. The canonical linear exponential Hawkes process admits a faster $O(N)$ recurrence, but prior work evaluates this recurrence sequentially, without exploiting parallelization on modern GPUs. We show that the Hawkes process intensity can be expressed as a product of sparse transition matrices admitting a linear-time associative multiply, enabling computation via a parallel prefix scan. This yields a simple yet massively parallelizable algorithm for maximum likelihood estimation of linear exponential Hawkes processes. Our method reduces the computational complexity to approximately $O(N/P)$ with $P$ parallel processors, and naturally yields a batching scheme to maintain constant memory usage, avoiding GPU memory constraints. Importantly, it computes the exact likelihood without any additional assumptions or approximations, preserving the simplicity and interpretability of the model. We demonstrate orders-of-magnitude speedups on simulated and real datasets, scaling to thousands of nodes and tens of millions of events, substantially beyond scales reported in prior work. We provide an open-source PyTorch library implementing our optimizations.

</details>


### [150] [Malliavin Calculus for Counterfactual Gradient Estimation in Adaptive Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01345)
*Vikram Krishnamurthy,Luke Snow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Malliavin微积分的被动Langevin算法，用于自适应逆强化学习，通过高效估计反事实梯度来重构前向学习者的损失函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应逆强化学习需要从观察到的梯度中重构前向学习者的损失函数，但所需的反事实梯度条件于零概率事件，传统蒙特卡洛估计效率低下，核平滑方法收敛缓慢。

Method: 采用Malliavin微积分将反事实条件化为涉及Malliavin量的无条件期望之比，推导Langevin结构的Malliavin导数及其伴随Skorohod积分形式，提出具体算法实现反事实梯度估计。

Result: 通过Malliavin微积分方法克服了反事实梯度估计的困难，恢复了标准估计速率，避免了传统方法的低效问题。

Conclusion: 提出的基于Malliavin微积分的被动Langevin算法为自适应逆强化学习提供了有效的反事实梯度估计方法，解决了传统方法效率低下的问题。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) recovers the loss function of a forward learner from its observed responses adaptive IRL aims to reconstruct the loss function of a forward learner by passively observing its gradients as it performs reinforcement learning (RL). This paper proposes a novel passive Langevin-based algorithm that achieves adaptive IRL. The key difficulty in adaptive IRL is that the required gradients in the passive algorithm are counterfactual, that is, they are conditioned on events of probability zero under the forward learner's trajectory. Therefore, naive Monte Carlo estimators are prohibitively inefficient, and kernel smoothing, though common, suffers from slow convergence. We overcome this by employing Malliavin calculus to efficiently estimate the required counterfactual gradients. We reformulate the counterfactual conditioning as a ratio of unconditioned expectations involving Malliavin quantities, thus recovering standard estimation rates. We derive the necessary Malliavin derivatives and their adjoint Skorohod integral formulations for a general Langevin structure, and provide a concrete algorithmic approach which exploits these for counterfactual gradient estimation.

</details>


### [151] [Residuals-based Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01378)
*Qing Zhu,Xian Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于残差的离线强化学习框架，通过显式纳入转移动态估计误差来缓解分布偏移问题，并在一般状态和动作空间上具有理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习在无需与环境交互的情况下从历史数据中学习策略，对高风险应用尤为重要。现有方法通常依赖严格的数据覆盖假设且易受分布偏移影响，需要更稳健的算法。

Method: 提出基于残差的离线RL框架，定义基于残差的贝尔曼最优算子，将转移动态估计误差显式纳入策略优化。该算子利用经验残差，是压缩映射，并在特定条件下具有渐近最优性和有限样本保证。进一步开发了基于残差的离线深度Q学习算法。

Result: 在随机CartPole环境中验证了基于残差的离线DQN算法的有效性，展示了该方法在实际应用中的可行性。

Conclusion: 基于残差的离线RL框架通过显式处理转移动态估计误差，缓解了分布偏移问题，为一般状态和动作空间提供了理论保证的解决方案，在高风险应用中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) has received increasing attention for learning policies from previously collected data without interaction with the real environment, which is particularly important in high-stakes applications. While a growing body of work has developed offline RL algorithms, these methods often rely on restrictive assumptions about data coverage and suffer from distribution shift. In this paper, we propose a residuals-based offline RL framework for general state and action spaces. Specifically, we define a residuals-based Bellman optimality operator that explicitly incorporates estimation error in learning transition dynamics into policy optimization by leveraging empirical residuals. We show that this Bellman operator is a contraction mapping and identify conditions under which its fixed point is asymptotically optimal and possesses finite-sample guarantees. We further develop a residuals-based offline deep Q-learning (DQN) algorithm. Using a stochastic CartPole environment, we demonstrate the effectiveness of our residuals-based offline DQN algorithm.

</details>


### [152] [Benchmark Problems and Benchmark Datasets for the evaluation of Machine and Deep Learning methods on Photoplethysmography signals: the D4 report from the QUMPHY project](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01398)
*Urs Hackstein,Jordi Alastruey,Philip Aston,Ciaran Bench,Peter H. Charlton,Loic Coquelin,Nando Hegemann,Vaidotas Marozas,Mohammad Moulaeifard,Manasi Nandi,Andrius Petrenas,Oskar Pfeffer,Mantas Rinkevicius,Andrius Solosenko,Nils Strodthoff,Sara Vardanega*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该报告介绍了Qumphy项目中用于评估机器学习算法在PPG信号分析中不确定性的六个医学基准问题及相关数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发量化机器学习算法在医学问题（特别是PPG信号分析）中不确定性的方法，为相关研究提供标准化评估框架。

Method: 识别并列出六个与PPG信号相关的医学问题作为基准问题，描述适用的基准数据集及其使用方法。

Result: 建立了包含六个PPG相关医学问题的基准问题集，并提供了相应数据集的详细说明和使用指南。

Conclusion: 该报告为评估机器学习算法在PPG信号分析中的不确定性提供了系统化的基准框架，有助于推动该领域的标准化研究。

Abstract: This report is part of the Qumphy project (22HLT01 Qumphy) that is funded by the European Union and is dedicated to the development of measures to quantify the uncertainties associated with Machine Learning algorithms applied to medical problems, in particular the analysis and processing of Photoplethysmography (PPG) signals. In this report, a list of six medical problems that are related to PPG signals and serve as Benchmark Problems is given. Suitable Benchmark datasets and their usage are described also.

</details>


### [153] [Test-Time Scaling Makes Overtraining Compute-Optimal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01411)
*Nicholas Roberts,Sungjun Cho,Zhiqi Gao,Tzu-Heng Huang,Albert Wu,Gabriel Orlanski,Avi Trost,Kelly Buchanan,Aws Albarghouthi,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了Train-to-Test（T²）缩放定律，在固定端到端预算下联合优化模型大小、训练token数和推理样本数，发现考虑推理成本时最优预训练决策会大幅转向过训练区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练缩放定律（如Chinchilla）未考虑测试时缩放（如重复采样）带来的推理成本，而现代LLMs在测试时会产生与模型大小和采样数量相关的成本，这需要在固定预算下联合优化预训练和测试时决策。

Method: 提出T²缩放定律，将预训练缩放定律现代化，加入用于测试时缩放的pass@k建模，然后在固定端到端预算下联合优化模型大小、训练token数和推理样本数。通过任务损失和任务准确性两种建模方法验证鲁棒性。

Result: 在八个下游任务上发现，当考虑推理成本时，最优预训练决策会大幅转向过训练区域，远超标准预训练缩放套件的范围。在T²预测的最优区域预训练过训练模型，验证了其性能显著优于仅基于预训练缩放的模型。后训练阶段的结果也支持这一发现。

Conclusion: T²缩放定律为现代LLM部署提供了更全面的优化框架，表明在考虑端到端成本时，过训练策略可能比传统预训练缩放定律建议的更优，这对实际部署具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Modern LLMs scale at test-time, e.g. via repeated sampling, where inference cost grows with model size and the number of samples. This creates a trade-off that pretraining scaling laws, such as Chinchilla, do not address. We present Train-to-Test ($T^2$) scaling laws that jointly optimize model size, training tokens, and number of inference samples under fixed end-to-end budgets. $T^2$ modernizes pretraining scaling laws with pass@$k$ modeling used for test-time scaling, then jointly optimizes pretraining and test-time decisions. Forecasts from $T^2$ are robust over distinct modeling approaches: measuring joint scaling effect on the task loss and modeling impact on task accuracy. Across eight downstream tasks, we find that when accounting for inference cost, optimal pretraining decisions shift radically into the overtraining regime, well-outside of the range of standard pretraining scaling suites. We validate our results by pretraining heavily overtrained models in the optimal region that $T^2$ scaling forecasts, confirming their substantially stronger performance compared to pretraining scaling alone. Finally, as frontier LLMs are post-trained, we show that our findings survive the post-training stage, making $T^2$ scaling meaningful in modern deployments.

</details>


### [154] [Improving Latent Generalization Using Test-time Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01430)
*Arslan Chaudhry,Sridhar Thiagarajan,Andrew Lampinen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究语言模型如何通过测试时计算（"思考"）来改善潜在泛化能力，使用强化学习训练模型生成长链式推理，解决知识内化学习中的推理缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型存在两种知识获取机制：权重内学习和上下文学习。权重内学习在对其内部知识进行演绎推理时存在困难，表现为潜在泛化缺陷（如逆转诅咒）。上下文学习虽然具有强大的潜在泛化能力，但需要依赖外部上下文。现有方法依赖训练时数据增强，但这些方法任务特定、扩展性差，且无法泛化到分布外知识。

Method: 使用基于正确性反馈的强化学习来训练模型生成长链式推理（CoTs），以改善潜在泛化能力。这种方法教导模型利用测试时计算（"思考"）来增强推理能力，而不是依赖训练时数据增强。

Result: 思考方法不仅解决了分布内知识上的许多潜在泛化失败实例，而且与增强基线不同，能够泛化到未进行强化学习的新知识。在纯逆转任务中，思考虽不能直接实现知识反转，但通过生成-验证能力使模型性能显著高于随机水平。然而，事实自我验证的脆弱性意味着思考模型在该任务上仍远低于上下文学习的性能。

Conclusion: 测试时思考是改善语言模型潜在泛化能力的灵活且有前景的方向，能够弥补权重内学习在演绎推理方面的缺陷，并展现出对分布外知识的泛化能力。

Abstract: Language Models (LMs) exhibit two distinct mechanisms for knowledge acquisition: in-weights learning (i.e., encoding information within the model weights) and in-context learning (ICL). Although these two modes offer complementary strengths, in-weights learning frequently struggles to facilitate deductive reasoning over the internalized knowledge. We characterize this limitation as a deficit in latent generalization, of which the reversal curse is one example. Conversely, in-context learning demonstrates highly robust latent generalization capabilities. To improve latent generalization from in-weights knowledge, prior approaches rely on train-time data augmentation, yet these techniques are task-specific, scale poorly, and fail to generalize to out-of-distribution knowledge. To overcome these shortcomings, this work studies how models can be taught to use test-time compute, or 'thinking', specifically to improve latent generalization. We use Reinforcement Learning (RL) from correctness feedback to train models to produce long chains-of-thought (CoTs) to improve latent generalization.
